# Classic → React parity evidence ledger

Audit baseline: checkpoint `3e2f95b5`, 2026-09-21. Phase 4 is **open**.

A classification is a product requirement/disposition, not a passing result. `Required` and `Redesigned equivalent` rows have executable acceptance scenarios below; existing code or a matching command string is only implementation evidence. Unless a named test/run is recorded, acceptance is **unverified**. `Missing` and `Present but behaviorally degraded` remain required repair work. No feature is silently retired.

Sources below are relative to `static/js/`; React owners are under `ui/src/`. All runtime acceptance uses an isolated profile and port. The live daemon must not be deployed, stopped or restarted.

## Behavior matrix

## Validation record

- Baseline: `make ui-check` passed (11 files, 102 tests, lint/typecheck/build and build scan).
- Baseline: `make lint-docs-contract` passed (71 Markdown files); `git diff --check` passed.

- Phase 4 and classic retirement remain blocked while required gaps or unverified product decisions remain.

## Settings field comparison

P-330/D-185 defines the save-boundary text contract across these fields: known commands, model/reasoning overrides, paths and task defaults use Classic outer-whitespace normalization. Empty worktree base/dispatch/journal fields use their explicit defaults. Instruction payloads and arbitrary maps remain exact; server normalizers still govern stored values. This does not certify every field or runtime effect.

Except for explicitly identified runtime and compatibility-only records, each row is a Required field contract, implemented by `StructuredSettings.tsx` or its explicit Settings panel control. Defaults are hydrated from the daemon; descriptions retain dataclass inheritance/unit notes. Opening the form waits for all settings responses. An unchanged inherited relay value is not promoted to an explicit override. Choices/min/max originate from the classic form and are server-validated on save. Daemon provider/model/reasoning choices and per-agent reset/override intent now have focused coverage. Daemon-backed section/field resets and sparse saves now have named coverage below. The original 163-row inventory includes runtime records and the classic `provider` alias; those are identified below rather than presented as editable defaults. Nested GitHub number/maps omitted by the original inventory are included. Exhaustive non-default boundary/enum and runtime-effect checks remain open; default write/reload and typed-control coverage do not certify those effects.

Acceptance for **each field**: hydrate its server value, change it using the listed control, save, reopen and compare; exercise its boundary/enum validation and verify unrelated incoming data preserves draft/focus. Generic control and coordinated-save tests cover shared mechanics, not every field's backend effect.

| Field | Scope | Control / choices | Description / constraints |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `default_command` | Global | Text | empty = use config.DEFAULT_COMMAND (env var fallback) |
| `filter_by_window` | Group, Global | Enabled/Disabled; Classic-only scope guidance | Active Classic grid filtering by focused terminal window; React selected-group scope is unchanged (D-213). |
| `focus_new_tabs` | Global | Compatibility-only · excluded from edit/reset | No current runtime consumer. Preserve stored values through unrelated edits and resets; D-213. |
| `focus_on_click` | Global | Enabled/Disabled | Live preference controls ownership-row terminal activation; P-332/D-186. |
| `max_pipeline_depth` | Global | Number | 0 = unlimited; min=0 |
| `max_event_log` | Global | Number | max persisted panel events; min=50, max=10000 |
| `github_repo` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `github_project_owner` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `github_project_status_field` | Classic settings | Text | Server value/default |
| `board_sync_enabled` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Server value/default |
| `provider` | Classic alias | Uses `board_sync_provider` | One group sync-provider control; not a CLI provider setting. |
| `default_agent_template` | Group | Scoped role/template selector | None; Project/User display names; unavailable saved value retained |
| `agent_provider` | Group | Text | adapter name ("claude-code", "codex", etc.) — empty = use default |
| `agent_boot_command` | Group | Text | override default boot command (e.g. "codex") |
| `agent_model` | Group | Text | default model override when provider supports it |
| `agent_reasoning_effort` | Group | Text | default reasoning-effort override |
| `agent_fast_mode` | Group | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `worker_provider` | Group | Text | adapter override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_boot_command` | Group | Text | boot command override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_model` | Group | Text | model override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_reasoning_effort` | Group | Text | reasoning override for workers (empty = use group default) |
| `worker_fast_mode` | Group | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `agent_directory` | Group | Text | Server value/default |
| `agent_shell` | Group | Select: inherit, zsh, bash, fish | Server value/default |
| `engineer_merge_mode` | Group | Select: pr, direct, engineer-choice | All values save/reopen; direct performs a local merge without an override, engineer-choice permits explicit direct merge, PR-only refuses it before external effects. External GitHub hosting is not certified here. |
| `worktree_merge_cleanup` | Group | Select: keep, auto_sweep, close, remove, close_remove | All five values save/reopen and reach actual cleanup. Attached-session removal and local squash branch preservation remain guarded with visible warnings; stopped-worker removal succeeds. |
| `engineer_provider` | Engineer | Text | adapter name override (empty = use group default) |
| `engineer_boot_command` | Engineer | Text | boot command override (empty = use provider default) |
| `engineer_model` | Engineer | Text | model override for the designated engineer |
| `engineer_reasoning_effort` | Engineer | Text | reasoning-effort override for the designated engineer |
| `engineer_fast_mode` | Engineer | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `engineer_directory` | Engineer | Text | directory override for the designated engineer |
| `engineer_shell` | Engineer | Select: inherit, zsh, bash, fish | shell override for the designated engineer |
| `custom_instructions` | Engineer | Text | user-defined instructions appended to engineer system prompt |
| `autonomy_mode` | Engineer | Select: suggest_only, dispatch_when_clear, aggressive_auto_continue | suggest_only \| dispatch_when_clear \| aggressive_auto_continue |
| `wave_size_preference` | Engineer | Select: small, balanced, large | small \| balanced \| large |
| `same_agent_follow_up_preference` | Engineer | Select: balanced, prefer_same_agent, prefer_fresh_agent | balanced \| prefer_same_agent \| prefer_fresh_agent |
| `digest_verbosity` | Engineer | Select: compact, balanced, detailed | compact \| balanced \| detailed |
| `escalation_style` | Engineer | Select: ask_early, note_then_ask, keep_moving | ask_early \| note_then_ask \| keep_moving |
| `architect_provider` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_boot_command` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_model` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_reasoning_effort` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_fast_mode` | Group, Architect | Select: inherit, on, off | Codex Fast: inherit \| on \| off |
| `architect_directory` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_shell` | Group, Architect | Select: inherit, zsh, bash, fish | Server value/default |
| `architect_custom_instructions` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `architect_autonomy_mode` | Group, Architect | Select: dispatch_freely, dispatch_after_confirm, ask_always | Server value/default |
| `architect_digest_verbosity` | Group, Architect | Select: terse, balanced, verbose | Server value/default |
| `architect_journal_checkpoint_frequency` | Group, Architect | Text | Server value/default |
| `push_interval` | Engineer | Select: 10, 30, 60, 120, 300 | seconds between digest pushes (min: 10) |
| `max_interval` | Engineer | Select: 60, 120, 300, 600 | max seconds between normal digest pushes |
| `heartbeat_interval` | Engineer | Select: 0, 60, 120, 300, 600 | quiet seconds before idle heartbeat digest (0 = off) |
| `default_directory` | Group | Text | Server value/default |
| `shell` | Group | Select: inherit, zsh, bash, fish | Server value/default |
| `env_vars` | Group | Nested named fields | Literal base environment; actual agent/terminal precedence and complete removal verified by environment launch scenarios. |
| `env_file` | Group | Text | Shell environment file fallback; saved/reopened path and actual agent/terminal file loading verified. |
| `max_agents` | Group | Number | Server value/default; min=0, max=100 |
| `collapsed_default` | Group | Enabled/Disabled; Classic-only scope guidance | Active Classic group disclosure default; distinct from React explicit agent-branch disclosure (D-209/D-213). |
| `agent_env_vars` | Group | Nested named fields | Overrides group variables; exact values reach Worker/Engineer/Architect processes. Removal restores group fallback; empty values remain literal. |
| `agent_env_file` | Group | Text | Overrides group environment file; saved/reopened paths and clearing-to-group fallback verified in actual launches. |
| `git_worktree` | Group | Worktrees: Enabled/Disabled | Both values save/reopen; enabled launches an isolated checkout, disabled launches in the actual group repository. |
| `worktree_base_dir` | Group | Text | Saved repository-relative path (including spaces) controls the actual checkout location; clearing restores `.torque/worktrees`. |
| `worktree_base_branch` | Group | Text | Saved named branch selects its actual commit; clearing uses current HEAD, verified with divergent fixture commits. |
| `worktree_merge_squash` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Saved values reach actual merges: one-parent squash excludes the two worker commits; two-parent merge retains both in ancestry. |
| `worktree_merge_preserve_diff` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Both values save/reopen; enabled creates an actual boundary patch with merge/task metadata, disabled leaves no preserved patch. |
| `worktree_symlink_gitignored_paths` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Actual ignored file/directory symlinks appear when enabled and remain absent when disabled unless explicitly listed. |
| `agent_session_resume` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | resume session on relaunch |
| `agent_idle_timeout` | Group | Number | minutes before flagging agent as stuck (0=disable); min=0, max=60 |
| `engineer_behavior_requires_user_approval` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Server value/default |
| `notifications` | Group | Desktop notifications: Enabled/Disabled | Adds macOS delivery independently of durable Inbox recording. Both values persist/reopen; batching policy has backend coverage, native banners are not certified by browser tests. |
| `notify_on_finish` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Both values save/reopen; real current-turn completion creates an Inbox notification only when enabled. |
| `notify_on_error` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Both values save/reopen; ingested agent error creates an Inbox alert only when enabled. |
| `notify_on_attention` | Group | Enabled/Disabled | Both values save/reopen; ingested waiting event creates an Inbox notification only when enabled. |
| `board_default_action` | Group | Text | default action for new tasks |
| `board_default_labels` | Group | One value per line | default labels for new tasks |
| `board_default_lane` | Group | Text | default lane for new tasks (empty = first lane) |
| `dispatch_lane` | Group | Text | lane for dispatched tasks |
| `board_sync_provider` | Group | Select: none, github | none \| github (future providers reserved) |
| `github_project_id` | Group `board_sync_github` | Text | Server value/default |
| `github_project_number` | Group `board_sync_github` | Number | Nonnegative integer; stored as a number. |
| `github_lane_status_map` | Group `board_sync_github` | Named text map | Lane to project-status mapping. |
| `github_assignee_map` | Group `board_sync_github` | Named text map | Agent to GitHub-assignee mapping. |

| Direct messages (`terminal/direct-messages.js`) | Copy code block | Required | Open from the appropriate entity by pointer and keyboard; exercise copy code block with valid and invalid state, verify exact target, preserve selection/draft, Escape and return focus. |
| Direct messages (`terminal/direct-messages.js`) | Reply to selected message | Required | Open from the appropriate entity by pointer and keyboard; exercise reply to selected message with valid and invalid state, verify exact target, preserve selection/draft, Escape and return focus. |
| Direct messages (`terminal/direct-messages.js`) | Cancel reply | Required | Open from the appropriate entity by pointer and keyboard; exercise cancel reply with valid and invalid state, verify exact target, preserve selection/draft, Escape and return focus. |
| Direct messages (`terminal/direct-messages.js`) | Keyboard message selection | Required | Open from the appropriate entity by pointer and keyboard; exercise keyboard message selection with valid and invalid state, verify exact target, preserve selection/draft, Escape and return focus. |

## Pointer/layout operation inventory

| Operation | Classification | Acceptance |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Root-agent position (`commands.js` ordering handlers) | Redesigned equivalent | Move/Reorder places a root before any eligible root and reload preserves it. |
| Owned-agent sibling position | Redesigned equivalent | Reorder inside its ownership tier without changing owner IDs. |
| Terminal sibling position | Redesigned equivalent | Dedicated reorder_child persists before/after placement and resync shows it. |
| Terminal attach/detach | Redesigned equivalent | reparent_terminal preserves terminal identity, parent/group rules and selection. |
| Group-tab ordering | Required | Pointer drop and keyboard Move group insert at beginning/middle/end, surviving reload. |
| Task card/subtree lane drop | Required | Move subtree within/across lanes; reject invalid derived-task placement and retain independent scroll. |
| Board lane reorder | Required | Reorder eligible lanes without orphaning tasks or changing hidden/selected preferences. |
| Task multi-selection bulk move | Required | Selected task set and destination survive filter/delta; only selected tasks move. |
| Artifact/file drop | Required | Drop files onto correct task/composer, inspect previews and remove before send. |
| Sidebar separator | Redesigned equivalent | Pointer and keyboard resize clamps width and persists it. |
| Terminal/DM separator | Redesigned equivalent | Pointer and keyboard resize clamps height and retains draft, caret and scroll. |
| Composer separator | Redesigned equivalent | Resize text region without stealing selection or sending a message. |
| Pipeline background pan | Required | Pointer capture continues outside graph until release/cancel; arrow keys pan without activating a node. |
| Canvas agent coordinates | Intentionally retired | D-077: ownership ordering replaces spatial positions; no persisted coordinate migration is claimed. |
| Dock-zone drag, tab insertion, split resize | Intentionally retired | D-077: fixed workspaces and explicit regional separators replace arbitrary docking. |
| Floating panel move, eight-edge resize, z-order | Intentionally retired | D-077: whole-workspace native windows replace separate in-app floating panels, with no arbitrary multi-panel composition. |
| Whole-workspace native bounds | Required | Resize/move detached window, reopen it and verify restored bounds on each supported platform. |
| Detached close/reattach ownership | Required | Source reclaims terminal only after native ownership returns; browser does not invoke native-only operations. |

## Worktree mutation acknowledgement checkpoint — 2026-09-22

- Inspector checkpoint, rollback, rebase, PR, merge and removal now use explicit keyed HTTP writes with a duplicate-pending guard. Confirmations and merge drafts remain after refusal; rollback/removal dismiss only after the requested target/result is confirmed. A lost/malformed/mismatched acknowledgement keeps the exact retry payload/key, has a bounded 120-second wait, and never replays automatically on reconnect. A known refusal starts a fresh key on the next explicit attempt.
- Accepted results refresh scoped reads; cached terminal merge progress cannot leave controls disabled. Pending PR links, merge errors and cleanup warnings remain visible. The submitted target survives an agent-closing merge long enough to show its result without granting further writes against an unavailable agent. D-145 records the contract and retry limitations.
- Explicit checkpoint now distinguishes a clean no-op from Git failure; automatic checkpoint callers retain their best-effort default. Rollback checks the actual manager result, and PR responses include the target/outcome. Missing removal targets return an explicit refusal. The HTTP coordinator is restricted to the six keyed worktree commands, joins overlapping retries even after a waiter disconnects, rejects a changed payload sharing a pending key, and preserves the existing completed-response cache and unrelated command paths. No crash/partial-side-effect atomicity is claimed.
- The browser rebase case exposed a pre-existing P-243 defect: valid blocked preflight data carrying `error` was discarded as a failed read, hiding Rebase. Shape-validated preflight refusals are now retained as domain data, exposing stale/conflict recovery and continuing to block PR/merge. This has a focused component regression and actual Git base-advance acceptance.

### 2026-09-23 — Settings navigation preserves drafts and pending saves

- P-253 routes section, workspace, group, command-palette, same-window Classic-link and native-menu navigation through a shared owner guard. The complete action waits for explicit discard; cancellation keeps the mounted form, editor focus/caret, values and local secret buffers without sending the requested navigation or any settings write. Discard clears unsaved state, including secret-entry buffers, and performs the requested action once. An unchanged or reverted draft leaves without a prompt.
- Pending saves keep Settings mounted and preserve the original scope and requested destination. The operator sees the result before deciding whether to leave; failed saves retain the draft, successful completion does not trigger automatic navigation, and already saved scopes are not rolled back by discarding remaining edits. The existing coordinated save transport is unchanged.
- Active-group changes from the daemon cannot silently replace an edited/saving form. A visible retained-group notice offers an explicit switch; the real browser run saved to the original group after another group became active and proved the other group's settings were untouched. External detached-window ownership changes also preserve the local form. Group context-menu Settings now uses the selected row's group.

They cover section/workspace cancellation, keyboard exit, exact-once group navigation, pending/refused/successful save transitions, native-menu and command-palette cancellation, discarded secrets, clean exits, external group changes and native detach gating/ownership. ### 2026-09-23 — Search and reveal settings without replacing drafts

- P-254 adds local search over mounted labels, associated descriptions and section/disclosure scopes, including closed controls and appearance toggle buttons. It never indexes draft values, option text or secret inputs. Results show their scopes, announce counts/empty state, mount 20 initially and expose Show more. Enter opens the first available result without submitting; Arrow Down enters results; Escape/Clear resets only search.

- P-287 implements mutually exclusive clear/replace/keep intent with explicit configured/new/clear status. Clearing removes the replacement draft; typing cancels clear; keeping does not resurrect discarded text. Only a typed AI settings acknowledgement clears password drafts. Refusal/malformed outcomes retain the draft for explicit retry, and save errors redact exact/trimmed submitted keys plus labeled credentials. Keys stay in mounted component state and never enter shared projection or browser storage.

Cancel retains edits and sends nothing. Backend-requested consent is owned by the requesting form rather than cached responses; changed drafts invalidate it, unrelated reconnect retains it, and successful save/close removes it. Partial non-AI acknowledgements remain recorded so confirming or retrying AI does not replay earlier writes. D-170 records both contracts.

### Live terminal appearance and scrollback checkpoint — 2026-09-25

- P-294/P-295 are implemented under D-173. New terminals use current document font/background/text/accent/selection tokens and normalized saved scrollback. Existing xterm instances observe appearance changes and consume saved global settings without remounting the controller or socket. Color-only changes avoid a fit; font changes use the established reading/tail-preserving fit and visible-owner geometry gate. Disposal disconnects the observer.
- Scrollback follows Classic's 100–100,000 bounds, flooring and 2,000-line fallback; the Settings fallback is aligned with the daemon's 2,000 default. Changes arrive from projection deltas, acknowledged settings reads and reconnect snapshots. Local unsaved settings drafts never change terminal history. Reducing the limit intentionally trims older history; surviving reading context remains in place.

### Planning request recovery source audit — 2026-09-26

- Source remains frozen at implementation checkpoint `d8129077` while the complete 116-scenario browser suite runs serially against isolated profile `react-full-parity-20260926`, port 19041. - P-334 is a confirmed source gap: `ThinkingEditor`, `usePlanningEditor` and `AreaEditor` call `readCommand` without an independent deadline. Their existing abort/identity checks handle ordinary replacement but cannot expose retry for an unanswered request. Top-level `usePlanningReads` already has a 30-second recovery timer; that collection-level implementation does not cover editor detail reads.
- P-335 is a confirmed source gap: `usePlanningMutation` uses an unretained controller, no deadline or unmount ownership check, and accepts any non-error frame before dependent writes or draft clearing. Area duplicates this path without an explicit frame-error check. Backend response contracts are inspectable in `torque/commands/planning.py` and `torque/mcp_scoped/dispatch_planning.py`; regression fixtures must match these contracts rather than accepting arbitrary success-shaped frames.
- P-336/P-337 are distinct confirmed source gaps in `InitiativeTaskCreator`: task-option reads have no deadline, expected-type check or abort guard before dispatch; the acknowledged-task link step has no deadline or lifecycle ownership. Preserve its existing known-task-ID recovery: a link retry must never create another Board task.
- These are source findings, not reproduced browser failures or completed repairs. Next acceptance work must exercise stalled and malformed responses, retained drafts, late/unmounted completion and dependent-command ordering for each mapped path. Full Planning relation/target combinations remain independently required.
- Main ledger: **337 behaviors: 325 implemented/equivalent dispositions, 8 open audits/repairs and 4 intentional retirements**. Open: P-087/P-088/P-089/P-112 and P-334–P-337. ### P-087 Global Settings: complete field reconciliation accepted (2026-09-30)

The actual `editableSettings` projection of the current daemon-contract fixture contains **33 Global fields**, independently rederived with no additions or omissions. The table names executable evidence for every field. Test filenames without a directory are under `ui/e2e/`; backend paths are explicit. The 67 Group and 42 principal fields are separate scopes, and inactive/runtime records remain preserved under the documented compatibility rules.

The row-level contract is typed UI editing, validation, save/reopen, inheritance/reset/recovery where applicable, and the downstream effect or focused backend consumer identified below. This does not certify hosted Relay connectivity, commercial AI inference, physical native notification delivery or other independent release gates. Those limits do not change the shared backend behavior of Classic versus React. Settings navigation/outcome/reconnect tests additionally cover scope ownership, exact partial retries and draft/focus/caret continuity.

| Global editable field | Executable acceptance | Verified contract |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `default_command` | `settings-default-command-live.spec.ts`; `settings-text-boundaries-live.spec.ts` | Trim/save/reload the global command; actual local Worker argv and directory prove inheritance and group override; clearing launches the runtime fallback without converting inheritance into an explicit edit. |
| `filter_by_window` | `settings-compatibility-live.spec.ts` | Both stored scope values and reset/preservation are verified with explicit Classic-only guidance. React intentionally keeps selected-group navigation; the active Classic consumer retains the preference. |
| `focus_on_click` | `agent-terminal-focus-live.spec.ts` | Both values are selected and saved in Settings, reopened, and changed live; real PTY input proves click/double-click/keyboard activation, retained composer drafts and focus across reconnect. |
| `xterm_scrollback` | `settings-validation-live.spec.ts`; `settings-outcomes-live.spec.ts`; `terminal-preferences-live.spec.ts` | Reject invalid/unsafe values; sparse save, refusal/deadline recovery and reload; actual xterm history shrinks/grows in place while preserving viewport and a single PTY connection. |
| `keybindings` | `keyboard-parity-live.spec.ts` | Reviewed conflicts, fixed/reserved combinations, reassignment, reset, reload and reconnect; effective hints and actual panel/task/composer keyboard actions use the saved mapping. |
| `max_pipeline_depth` | `settings-global-policy-live.spec.ts`; `settings-validation-live.spec.ts` | Saved positive limit refuses an over-depth derive with parent evidence; zero permits the same continuation. Numeric boundaries reject lossy drafts. |
| `max_event_log` | `settings-global-policy-live.spec.ts`; `settings-validation-live.spec.ts` | Positive bounded save/reload; lowering the limit trims the exact persisted event IDs immediately; increasing it admits new history without resurrecting deleted rows. |

The reconciliation added `settings-default-command-live.spec.ts` and strengthened `agent-terminal-focus-live.spec.ts` and `settings-relay-config-live.spec.ts`. Initial test assumptions incorrectly treated the global inherited command as a materialized creation-field value and expected a control inside a closed disclosure to be discoverable by visible role after reload. The corrected tests keep the inherited field empty, verify actual launched commands/process output and reopen the named disclosure before checking values. No production behavior was changed to accommodate the assertions. The initial evidence remains at `/private/tmp/settings-field-gap-initial-artifacts-20260930`.

Focused backend verification passed **127 tests** across AI configuration/index/summary, Relay resolution/start gating, event-ingest retention, agent-launch resolution and the daemon-to-React Settings contract (`/private/tmp/global-field-focused-backend-final-20260930.log`). The final set adds two direct consumer checks: each of the five persisted corpus switches selects the actual indexed source type, and disabling boot summaries suppresses automatic generation/public summary text while preserving the cached row for later enablement. Both new tests also passed independently (`/private/tmp/global-field-consumers-20260930.log`). Final `make ui-check` passed lint/typecheck, **113 files / 1,057 tests**, production build and bundle verification (`/private/tmp/global-fields-ui-check-20260930.log`). The subsequent frozen production-browser batch passed **27/27 scenarios in 4.2 minutes**, with no skips (`/private/tmp/global-fields-browser-final-20260930.log`). The actual global/group command receiver output and offline AI job transitions were inspected, together with the resolved Relay screenshot; artifacts are archived in `/private/tmp/global-fields-final-artifacts-20260930`. Browser inventory is **191 tests in 122 files**; this is inventory, not a complete-suite pass. The isolated profile on port 19092 has zero PTYs and is retained for the next Group audit; its fresh runtime identity is recorded in `/private/tmp/global-fields-runtime-checked-20260930.json`. Full backend, complete browser and native suites were not repeated because this checkpoint changes tests and acceptance documentation only. **P-087 is closed.** Counts are **381 mapped / 375 implemented or equivalent / two broad open / four intentional retirements**. P-088 Group Settings and P-112 consolidated lazy/reconnect remain open, together with the independent inventory/recovery/full-browser/native and Phase 4 gates.

The audit strengthened GitHub acceptance with typed round-trip/clearing for every nested field and both provider/enablement values, plus direct backend checks of the sync gate and assignee mapping. The new browser scenario passed independently (1/1), the two new consumer tests passed, and the complete focused sync/settings-contract set passed **75 tests** (`/private/tmp/group-github-focused-backend-20260930.log`). The consolidated browser run passed **29/30 scenarios in 9.5 minutes**; the failure was a stale environment-test assumption that Engineer/Architect creation still exposes the Worker worktree checkbox removed by P-379. The corrected scenario asserts that control only for Workers and its absence for principals, and then passed all six actual environment launches. This is a corrected test assumption, not a newly changed product behavior. The original run and trace are preserved in `/private/tmp/group-fields-browser-final-20260930.log` and `/private/tmp/group-fields-initial-artifacts-20260930`; the corrected environment pass is recorded in `/private/tmp/group-reconnect-corrections-20260930.log` and `/private/tmp/group-reconnect-corrections-initial-artifacts-20260930`. Thus all 30 Group scenarios have passing evidence across the two runs; a single uninterrupted 30/30 run is not claimed. The actual environment, checkpoint, worktree and merge JSON was inspected.

Final `make ui-check` passed lint/typecheck, **113 files / 1,057 tests**, build and bundle verification (`/private/tmp/group-reconnect-final-ui-check-20260930.log`). All nine production build files are byte-identical to the browser-tested build, verified by SHA-256 (`/private/tmp/group-fields-production-build-verified-20260930.json`). Documentation contracts and whitespace checks passed. The owned isolated QA profile on port 19092 has zero PTYs and is retained for the reconnect batch (`/private/tmp/group-fields-runtime-checked-20260930.json`). **P-088 is closed.** Ledger: **381 mapped / 376 implemented or equivalent / one broad open / four intentionally retired**. P-112 consolidated lazy/reconnect and independent inventory/recovery/full-browser/native/Phase 4 gates remain open. No production behavior or durable UX decision changed; full backend, complete browser and native suites were not repeated.


### P-112 consolidated lazy/reconnect reconciliation (2026-09-30)

The source audit covers the App workspace boundary, all eleven Control Center sections, Planning sections and editor dependencies, role-specific Agent Activity tabs, Live terminals, and the separate Worktree inspector. `App.tsx` mounts the active workspace and respects detached ownership; Planning and Control additionally use React lazy imports. Control mounts one section at a time. Planning/Activity scope reads to the active section/tab. Snapshot-projected surfaces refresh from the reconnect snapshot and need no artificial dedicated request. Shared connection/status subscriptions are not background-panel reads.

| Surface | Consolidated acceptance files under `ui/e2e/` |
| --- | --- |
| Board detail, reading windows and editor catalogs | `board-entry-recovery-live.spec.ts`; `board-read-recovery-live.spec.ts`; `board-catalog-recovery-live.spec.ts` |
| Role-specific Agent Activity and Agent Class details | `activity-lazy-live.spec.ts`; `activity-class-details-live.spec.ts` |
| Active Live terminal | `agent-terminal-focus-live.spec.ts`; `terminal-preferences-live.spec.ts` |
| Worktree inspector | `worktree-diff-live.spec.ts` |
| Planning sections, editor dependencies and Schedules | `planning-lazy-live.spec.ts`; `schedules-live.spec.ts` |
| Mission summary | `mission-control-live.spec.ts` |
| Health details/history | `health-live.spec.ts` |

### Shell confirmation gap and group lifecycle audit (2026-09-30)

Independent group UI audit found four shell actions calling `host.confirm` even though browserHost rejects native-confirm. Group removal, daemon restart/stop and the native supervisor callback therefore lacked the shared custom confirmation required by Classic parity. Group removal also described nonexistent protection gates, despite the backend cascading agents, child terminals and sessions. P-382–P-385 and D-228 record these separate behaviors. The original regression run failed all four scenarios: group/supervisor could not find a React dialog, while daemon controls exposed the missing accessible workspace-menu name. After implementation, the four focused scenarios pass; final UI and live acceptance remain pending.

The new live group test covers UI creation/default directory, rename, arbitrary menu ordering, actual drag/drop, reload persistence, removal cancellation/focus restoration, and confirmed deletion of an owned local Worker and child terminal. Lifecycle tests intercept daemon restart/stop/supervisor commands and assert exact reviewed payloads without executing them. Pending ledger: **385 mapped / 377 implemented or equivalent / four open / four intentionally retired**. Independent command/modal behavioral reconciliation, complete-browser and applicable native acceptance remain unproven.


Shell/browser acceptance passed **2/2 in 12.5 seconds** (`/private/tmp/group-shell-lifecycle-browser-corrected-20260930.log`). The live group scenario verifies create/default-directory persistence, rename, arbitrary menu ordering, real pointer drop and reload. It cancels removal by Escape and Cancel, restores menu focus, then removes exactly the reviewed group with its actual Worker/child terminal and verifies supervisor session count returns to baseline. Both other owned groups remain until teardown. The lifecycle scenario verifies all three exact commands, initial Cancel focus, Escape/Cancel without sends and reload without replay; those lifecycle commands are intercepted, never forwarded to the daemon. Component regressions cover native entry and disconnected send retention/explicit retry. The accessible confirmation screenshot was inspected; artifacts are `/private/tmp/group-shell-lifecycle-artifacts-20260930`. **P-382–P-385 are closed.**

The command audit then found P-386: Classic supports an optional note alongside external status, while React's Integrations Push always sends an empty note. This is a source-confirmed missing control, with focused reproduction/repair pending. A reference scan of all 191 commands found 41 with no literal occurrence in UI tests; these are audit leads, not 41 failures. For example, `architect_decision_link` is already exercised by label-driven Planning browser tests with actual persisted links. Audit reports: `/private/tmp/parity-command-test-reference-audit-20260930.json` and `/private/tmp/parity-command-audit-next-20260930.md`. Current ledger: **386 mapped / 381 implemented or equivalent / one confirmed open repair / four intentionally retired**. Full parity remains unproven until the remaining behavioral inventory and current complete-browser/applicable-native gates are reconciled.

### Organization acceptance and child-order persistence (2026-09-30; acceptance pending)

Source reconciliation confirms that Classic group moves preserve owner IDs and move attached terminals with their parent; owned Workers are not moved with their Engineer. The new actual-browser organization scenario passed in **10.5 seconds**, covering arbitrary root placement/end placement, owned Worker sibling ordering, standalone-terminal attachment, child ordering, cross-group reparenting, explicit detach, cancellation, parent/Worker group separation and rejoining, and browser reload with unchanged IDs and all eleven session IDs. The initial fixture was correctly refused because dispatched owned Workers require an action binding; the corrected fixture saves an action only inside its temporary local project. Logs: `/private/tmp/agent-organization-browser-20260930.log` (fixture refusal) and `/private/tmp/agent-organization-browser-action-20260930.log` (pass). Screenshot and state/command evidence were inspected; pre-persistence artifacts are `/private/tmp/agent-organization-live-artifacts-before-persistence-20260930`.

The explicit SQLite reload check then failed: child order reverted from third/first to first/third despite the reorder handler's persistence comment. This shared backend defect affects both UI callers and contradicts the ledger's saved-order acceptance, so P-387 remains open until repaired and verified. The fix persists the existing child index and reconciles it against authoritative parent IDs during daemon, bulk-snapshot and offline CLI reads. Focused verification passed **149 tests**, including the formerly failing database reload, malformed/cross-parent/duplicate rank recovery, bulk snapshot/CLI agreement and deferred-write argument capture (`/private/tmp/child-order-backend-focused-20260930.log`). Full regression and post-fix browser/SQLite acceptance are pending. Ledger: **387 mapped / 382 implemented or equivalent / one open repair / four intentionally retired**. Independent behavioral coverage and full browser/applicable native acceptance remain open.

The continuing command audit found **P-388**: Classic dismisses attention by task/agent ID and the item's source timestamp, then filters against persisted `events_dismissed_attention`. React currently attaches that action to a historical log row with the log ID/current time and never reads the dismissal map. Thus neither existing persisted dismissals nor new correct attention dismissal work. Repair and focused/live acceptance are next. Current ledger: **388 mapped / 383 implemented or equivalent / one open repair / four intentionally retired**. The full objective remains active.
